  private Optional<Locked> lock(Operation seen,boolean skip){
   var current=execution(seen.id(),seen.execution(),false,false);if(current.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();
   var task=q.row(current.orElseThrow().task(),false,false);if(task.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();
   if(!q.policies.containsKey(task.orElseThrow().spec().type()))return Optional.empty();
   var domain=q.lockDomain(task.orElseThrow().spec(),skip);if(domain.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();
   var heldTask=q.row(task.orElseThrow().id(),true,skip);if(heldTask.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();
   var op=operation(seen.id(),true,skip);if(op.isEmpty()||op.orElseThrow().execution()!=seen.execution())return Optional.empty();
   var ex=execution(seen.id(),seen.execution(),true,skip);if(ex.isEmpty()||!ex.orElseThrow().task().equals(heldTask.orElseThrow().id()))return Optional.empty();
   return Optional.of(new Locked(op.orElseThrow(),ex.orElseThrow(),heldTask.orElseThrow(),domain.orElseThrow()));

+ private static boolean observable(String state){return state.equals("MARKED")||state.equals("UNKNOWN");}
+ private Optional<OperationToken> claimCandidate(ScanCandidate candidate,String owner){return q.work(()->{
+  var initial=scanOperation(candidate.operationId(),false);if(initial.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();var discovered=initial.orElseThrow();var seen=discovered.operation();

+  if(!observable(seen.state())||!discovered.due())return Optional.empty();
+  var current=execution(seen.id(),seen.execution(),false,false);if(current.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();var seenExecution=current.orElseThrow();
+  var task=q.row(seenExecution.task(),false,false);if(task.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();var seenTask=task.orElseThrow();
+  if(!q.policies.containsKey(seenTask.spec().type()))return Optional.empty();
+  var scope=q.prepareDomain(List.of(seenTask.spec()),TaskDomainPolicy.Acquisition.TASK_MUTATION,true,null);if(scope.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();
+  if(q.lockDomain(scope.orElseThrow(),seenTask.spec(),true).isEmpty())return Optional.empty();
+  var heldTask=q.row(seenTask.id(),true,true);if(heldTask.isEmpty()||!heldTask.orElseThrow().spec().equals(seenTask.spec()))return Optional.empty();
+  q.policy(heldTask.orElseThrow().spec()).validateInput(heldTask.orElseThrow().spec());
+  var locked=scanOperation(seen.id(),true);if(locked.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();var latest=locked.orElseThrow();var operation=latest.operation();
+  if(latest.sequence()!=candidate.sequence())throw new IllegalStateException("Reconciliation scan ordinal changed");
+  if(operation.execution()!=seen.execution()||!operation.subject().equals(seen.subject())||!operation.subject().equals(heldTask.orElseThrow().spec().subject()))return Optional.empty();
+  var heldExecution=execution(operation.id(),operation.execution(),true,true);if(heldExecution.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();var execution=heldExecution.orElseThrow();
+  if(!execution.id().equals(seenExecution.id())||!execution.operation().equals(operation.id())||execution.number()!=operation.execution()||!execution.task().equals(heldTask.orElseThrow().id()))return Optional.empty();
+  Instant now=q.now();
+  if(!observable(operation.state())||!observable(execution.state())||operation.expiry()==null||latest.nextCheck()==null||operation.expiry().isAfter(now)||latest.nextCheck().isAfter(now))return Optional.empty();
+  Instant expiry=now.plusSeconds(20);
+  j.update("UPDATE external_operation SET reconcile_epoch=reconcile_epoch+1,reconcile_owner=?,reconcile_expires_at=?,next_check_at=? WHERE id=?",owner,sqlTime(expiry),sqlTime(expiry),operation.id());

  @Override public boolean renew(OperationToken token){independent();return q.work(()->{
   var seen=operation(token.operationId(),false,false);if(seen.isEmpty())return false;var rows=lock(seen.orElseThrow(),false);if(rows.isEmpty())return false;var l=rows.orElseThrow();if(!valid(l.op(),l.execution(),token))return false;
   j.update("UPDATE external_operation SET reconcile_expires_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)+INTERVAL 20 SECOND,next_check_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)+INTERVAL 20 SECOND WHERE id=?",token.operationId());return true;
  });}
  private static final int OBSERVATION_DISCOVERY_ATTEMPTS=4;
  private static final class RediscoverObservation extends RuntimeException {}
  @Override public ObservationResult observe(OperationToken token,ExternalObservation observation,Runnable effects){
   independent();revokeCallAuthority(token);
   for(int attempt=0;attempt<OBSERVATION_DISCOVERY_ATTEMPTS;attempt++){
    try{return q.work(()->observeCurrent(token,observation,effects));}
    catch(RediscoverObservation changed){
     // q.work has rolled back and released every subject/task/operation lock and the RR ReadView.
     // Never discover and lock a newly current task while retaining the previous operation lock.
    }
   }
   throw new ObservationRetryRequiredException();
  }
  private ObservationResult observeCurrent(OperationToken token,ExternalObservation observation,Runnable effects){
   var seen=operation(token.operationId(),false,false);if(seen.isEmpty())return ObservationResult.STALE_TOKEN;
   // Lock ALL involved tasks before operation when handling an old-generation contradiction.
   var current=seen.orElseThrow();var old=execution(current.id(),token.executionNo(),false,false);
   if(old.isEmpty()||!old.orElseThrow().id().equals(token.executionId())||!current.subject().equals(token.subject()))return ObservationResult.STALE_TOKEN;
   var currentEx=execution(current.id(),current.execution(),false,false).orElseThrow();
   var currentTask=q.row(currentEx.task(),false,false).orElseThrow();var oldTask=q.row(old.orElseThrow().task(),false,false).orElseThrow();
   var scope=q.prepareDomain(List.of(currentTask.spec(),oldTask.spec()),TaskDomainPolicy.Acquisition.EXTERNAL_FACT_OBSERVATION,false,null)
     .orElseThrow(DomainPreparationUnavailableException::new);
   var domain=q.lockDomain(scope,currentTask.spec(),false).orElseThrow(DomainPreparationUnavailableException::new);
   var heldTasks=new HashMap<String,JdbcTaskQueue.Row>();
   for(String id:new TreeSet<>(List.of(currentTask.id(),oldTask.id())))heldTasks.put(id,q.row(id,true,false).orElseThrow());
   var lockedCurrent=heldTasks.get(currentTask.id());var lockedOld=heldTasks.get(oldTask.id());
   if(!lockedCurrent.spec().equals(currentTask.spec())||!lockedOld.spec().equals(oldTask.spec()))throw new RediscoverObservation();
   currentTask=lockedCurrent;oldTask=lockedOld;
   var o=operation(current.id(),true,false).orElseThrow();
   if(o.execution()!=current.execution())throw new RediscoverObservation();
   var heldExecutions=new HashMap<Long,Execution>();
   for(long number:new TreeSet<>(List.of(o.execution(),token.executionNo())))
    heldExecutions.put(number,execution(o.id(),number,true,false).orElseThrow());
   var e=heldExecutions.get(o.execution());var tokenExecution=heldExecutions.get(token.executionNo());
   if(!e.task().equals(currentTask.id())||!tokenExecution.task().equals(oldTask.id()))throw new RediscoverObservation();
   if(!tokenExecution.id().equals(token.executionId())||!o.subject().equals(token.subject()))return ObservationResult.STALE_TOKEN;
   boolean effectsAllowed=scope.policy().permitsDomainEffects(scope.prepared());
   // A current authoritative fact can outlive business authority. Identity fencing cannot make
   // that fact permanently unrecordable merely because the disabled principal cannot renew.
   // Exact current execution and live observation states are still required; no lease is extended.
   boolean terminalFact=observation.outcome()==ExternalOutcome.SUCCEEDED||observation.outcome()==ExternalOutcome.FAILED_ZERO_EFFECT;
   boolean factOnlyCurrent=terminalFact&&!effectsAllowed&&o.execution()==token.executionNo()&&e.id().equals(token.executionId())
     &&(o.state().equals("MARKED")||o.state().equals("UNKNOWN"))&&(e.state().equals("MARKED")||e.state().equals("UNKNOWN"));
   if(!valid(o,e,token)&&!factOnlyCurrent){
    if(observation.outcome()==ExternalOutcome.SUCCEEDED&&tokenExecution.state().equals("FAILED")
       &&tokenExecution.basis()!=null&&tokenExecution.evidence()!=null&&tokenExecution.verified()!=null){
     q.policy(oldTask.spec()).validateExternalObservation(oldTask.spec(),observation);
     j.update("UPDATE external_operation SET state='MANUAL_REQUIRED',last_error='CONTRADICTORY_SUCCESS',external_result_id=?,reconcile_epoch=reconcile_epoch+1 WHERE id=?",observation.externalResultId(),o.id());
     q.event("EXTERNAL_CONTRADICTION",oldTask);return ObservationResult.QUARANTINED;
    }
    return ObservationResult.STALE_TOKEN;
   }
   q.policy(currentTask.spec()).validateExternalObservation(currentTask.spec(),observation);
   if(observation.outcome()==ExternalOutcome.UNKNOWN||observation.outcome()==ExternalOutcome.MANUAL_REQUIRED){
    j.update("UPDATE external_operation SET state=?,last_error=?,next_check_at=reconcile_expires_at WHERE id=?",observation.outcome()==ExternalOutcome.UNKNOWN?"UNKNOWN":"MANUAL_REQUIRED",observation.errorCode(),o.id());
    j.update("UPDATE external_execution SET state='UNKNOWN' WHERE id=?",e.id());q.event("EXTERNAL_UNKNOWN",currentTask);return ObservationResult.APPLIED;
   }
   boolean canApply=effectsAllowed&&valid(o,e,token)&&q.current(domain,currentTask.spec());
   if(canApply){q.policy(currentTask.spec()).validateOutput(currentTask.spec(),new TaskResult(observation.outcome()==ExternalOutcome.SUCCEEDED?Outcome.SUCCEEDED:Outcome.FAILED,observation.output(),observation.errorCode()));effects.run();}
   String state=observation.outcome()==ExternalOutcome.SUCCEEDED?"SUCCEEDED":"FAILED";
   var proof=observation.noEffectProof();
   j.update("UPDATE external_execution SET state=?,finished_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),no_effect_basis=?,no_effect_evidence=?,no_effect_verified_at=? WHERE id=?",state,proof==null?null:proof.basis().name(),proof==null?null:proof.safeEvidenceReference(),proof==null?null:sqlTime(proof.verifiedAt()),e.id());
   j.update("UPDATE external_operation SET state=?,external_result_id=?,last_error=? WHERE id=?",state,observation.externalResultId(),observation.errorCode(),o.id());
   q.finish(currentTask,canApply?state:"STALE",canApply?observation.errorCode():"BUSINESS_FENCED",canApply?observation.output():null);
   return canApply?ObservationResult.APPLIED:ObservationResult.RECORDED_FENCED;
  }
  @Override public String retryAfterZeroEffect(String id,long expected,TaskSpec next,ExternalRequest request){return q.work(()->{
   // Discover the COMPLETE immutable old/new spec set before identities, subject, roots or tasks.
   var seen=operation(id,false,false).orElseThrow();

   var replay=j.query("SELECT * FROM durable_task WHERE producer_key=? FOR UPDATE",JdbcTaskQueue::read,next.producerKey());
   if(!replay.isEmpty()){

+package ama.task;
+
+/** Logical discovery bounds per call; deployment tuning, never task or send authority. */
+public record ReconciliationScanBudget(int pageSize, int maxRowsPerCall) {
+    public ReconciliationScanBudget {
+        if (pageSize <= 0 || maxRowsPerCall < pageSize)
+            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Positive page size no larger than row budget required");
+    }
+}

+package ama.task;
+import ama.contract.ExternalContracts.OperationToken;
+import java.sql.*;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;
+import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
+
+class Task08FairnessIT {
+ @Test void moreThan64HeldIdentitiesProgressAcrossCalls()throws Exception{run(true);}
+ @Test void moreThan64UnregisteredPoliciesDoNotStarveRegisteredTail()throws Exception{run(false);}
+ static JdbcExternalOperations scanner(JdbcTaskQueue q){return new JdbcExternalOperations(q,new ReconciliationScanBudget(16,32));}
+ void run(boolean held)throws Exception{
+  String target=held?"fairheld":"fairunregistered",block=held?"T08_HELD":"T08_ABSENT",tailType="T08_TAIL";
+  var f=new Task08Fixture(target,block);
+  List<String> blockers=f.ids(block),tails=f.ids(tailType);
+  if(blockers.isEmpty()){
+   assertTrue(tails.isEmpty());
+   for(int i=0;i<129;i++)f.marker(f.spec(block));f.marker(f.spec(tailType));
+   blockers=f.ids(block);tails=f.ids(tailType);
+  }
+  assertEquals(129,blockers.size());assertEquals(1,tails.size());String tail=tails.getFirst();
+  assertEquals(130,f.j.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM external_operation",Integer.class));
+  for(String id:blockers)f.due(id);f.due(tail);
+  List<String> affected=List.of("A-t08-fair-union","\uE000-t08-fair-union");
+  if(held){for(String id:affected)if(f.j.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM enterprise_identity WHERE enterprise_id=?",Integer.class,id)==0)f.j.update("INSERT INTO enterprise_identity(enterprise_id,display_name,status,verified_at) VALUES (?,?,'ACTIVE',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",id,id);f.affected.addAll(affected);}
+  String before=f.skippedSnapshot(tail);
+  var scan=scanner(held?f.queue(block,tailType):f.queue(tailType));
+  try(Connection lock=f.source.getConnection()){
+   lock.setAutoCommit(false);
+   if(held){try(var stmt=lock.prepareStatement("SELECT status FROM enterprise_identity WHERE enterprise_id=? FOR UPDATE")){for(String id:blockers){stmt.setString(1,f.owner(id));try(var rows=stmt.executeQuery()){assertTrue(rows.next());}}}}
+   f.source.clear();Optional<OperationToken> claimed=Optional.empty();int calls=0;

+   var trace=List.copyOf(f.source.sql);

+   assertTrue(claimed.isPresent(),"129 "+(held?"held identities":"unregistered policies")+" must not starve later eligible operation by call5");
+   assertEquals(tail,claimed.orElseThrow().operationId());assertEquals(5,calls,"Explicit P16/B32 consumes at most32 rows/call");assertFalse(scan.hasCallAuthority(claimed.orElseThrow()));
+   assertEquals(before,f.skippedSnapshot(tail),"Complete skipped business/identity/task/execution/outbox bytes unchanged");
+   List<String> locks=trace.stream().map(Task08ProbeSource.Sql::text).filter(s->s.contains("FOR UPDATE")).toList();
+   if(!held)assertEquals(3,locks.stream().filter(s->s.contains("FROM durable_task")).count()+locks.stream().filter(s->s.contains("FROM external_operation")).count()+locks.stream().filter(s->s.contains("FROM external_execution")).count(),"Only registered tail acquires task/operation/execution");
+   if(held){
+    var identity=trace.stream().filter(s->s.text().contains("FROM enterprise_identity")&&s.text().contains("FOR UPDATE")).toList();assertEquals(129*2+3,identity.size());
+    assertEquals("A-t08-fair-union",identity.get(identity.size()-3).parameters().get(1));assertEquals("\uE000-t08-fair-union",identity.getLast().parameters().get(1));
+    int firstSubject=-1,lastIdentity=-1;for(int i=0;i<trace.size();i++){var s=trace.get(i).text();if(s.contains("FROM enterprise_identity")&&s.contains("FOR UPDATE"))lastIdentity=i;if(firstSubject<0&&s.contains("FROM task_subject"))firstSubject=i;}
+    assertTrue(lastIdentity<firstSubject,"Complete identity union before subject, blocked prefix got no roots");
+    try(var free=f.source.getConnection();var st=free.prepareStatement("SELECT enterprise_id FROM enterprise_identity WHERE enterprise_id=? FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED")){free.setAutoCommit(false);st.setString(1,affected.getFirst());try(var rows=st.executeQuery()){assertTrue(rows.next(),"Partial union released after every skipped candidate");}free.rollback();}
+   }
+   lock.rollback();
+  }
+  if(held)assertEquals(blockers.getFirst(),scan.claimReconciliation("T08_RELEASED").orElseThrow().operationId(),"Next pass revisits released prefix");
+ }

+package ama.task;

+/**
+ * B06 prerequisite composition proof only. The deliberately narrow policy uses the current
+ * lockRoot extension and actual B03 access. It is not a production meeting policy or runtime
+ * producer: TEST_INFO points to a real info row, never a fabricated transcription manifest.
+ */
+class Task08InheritedB06QueueSeamsIT {
+    final DataSource actual=Task08InheritedResources.source();
+    final List<String> trace=new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
+    final AtomicReference<Thread> completer=new AtomicReference<>();
+    volatile CountDownLatch completionAtIdentity;
+    final DataSource source=new ProbeSource(actual,sql->{
+        if(sql.contains("FOR UPDATE"))trace.add(Thread.currentThread().getName()+" "+sql);
+        if(Thread.currentThread()==completer.get() && sql.contains("FROM enterprise_identity")
+                && sql.contains("FOR UPDATE") && completionAtIdentity!=null)completionAtIdentity.countDown();
+    });
+    final JdbcTemplate j=new JdbcTemplate(source);
+    final TransactionRunner tx=new TransactionRunner(source);
+    final JdbcSubjectFences fences=new JdbcSubjectFences(source,Set.of("MEETING"));
+    final JdbcMeetingAccess access=new JdbcMeetingAccess(source,tx,fences);
+    final String owner="t08-b06-seam-"+UUID.randomUUID();
+    final Actor actor=new Actor(owner,Status.ACTIVE,Set.of(Role.USER));
+    final String meeting=UUID.randomUUID().toString(),input=UUID.randomUUID().toString();
+    final String taskType="T08_SEAM_"+UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-",""),otherTaskType="T08_OTHER_"+UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-","");
+    final TaskSpec spec=new TaskSpec(taskType,"B06_SEAM_"+UUID.randomUUID(),
+            new SubjectRef("MEETING",meeting),Task08InheritedFixture.hash(input+"producer"),
+            new TypedRef("TEST_INFO",input),Task08InheritedFixture.hash(input),0,0,0,3);
+    final SeamPolicy policy=new SeamPolicy();
+    final class SeamPolicy implements TaskDomainPolicy {
+        final JdbcTaskPreparations preparations=new JdbcTaskPreparations(source);
+        final AtomicInteger prepares=new AtomicInteger(),rootBatches=new AtomicInteger();
+        final List<String> affected=new ArrayList<>();
+        public Optional<PreparedDomain> prepare(List<TaskSpec> specs,Acquisition purpose,boolean skip){
+            prepares.incrementAndGet();
+            if(purpose==Acquisition.EXTERNAL_FACT_OBSERVATION){
+                var ids=new TreeSet<String>((a,b)->Arrays.compareUnsigned(a.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8),b.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
+                ids.add(owner);ids.addAll(affected);boolean allowed=true;
+                for(String id:ids){
+                    var statuses=j.queryForList("SELECT status FROM enterprise_identity WHERE enterprise_id=? FOR UPDATE"+(skip?" SKIP LOCKED":""),String.class,id);
+                    if(statuses.isEmpty())return Optional.empty();
+                    allowed&=statuses.getFirst().equals("ACTIVE");
+                }
+                return Optional.of(preparations.issue(specs,purpose,"FACT_ONLY",allowed));
+            }
+            var identities=access.tryLockIdentities(actor,affected,skip);
+            return identities.map(value->preparations.issue(specs,purpose,value,true));
+        }
+        public void requirePrepared(List<TaskSpec> specs,Acquisition purpose,PreparedDomain held){preparations.require(specs,purpose,held);}
+        public boolean permitsDomainEffects(PreparedDomain held){return preparations.permitsEffects(held);}
+        PreparedDomain adopt(TaskSpec s,MeetingAccess.Locked held){
+            access.requirePrepared(held,actor,s.subject().id(),affected,MeetingAccess.Action.EDIT);
+            return preparations.issue(List.of(s),Acquisition.TASK_MUTATION,held,true);
+        }
+        @Override public boolean lockRoots(List<TaskSpec> specs,boolean skip,PreparedDomain held){
+            rootBatches.incrementAndGet();
+            if(!TaskDomainPolicy.super.lockRoots(specs,skip,held))return false;
+            for(String id:new TreeSet<>(specs.stream().map(x->x.input().id()).toList()))
+                if(j.queryForList("SELECT id FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE"+(skip?" SKIP LOCKED":""),String.class,id).isEmpty())return false;
+            return true;
+        }
+        @Override public boolean lockRoot(TaskSpec s,boolean skip){throw new IllegalStateException("Explicit preparation required");}
+        @Override public boolean lockRoot(TaskSpec s,boolean skip,PreparedDomain prepared){
+            Object value=preparations.value(prepared);
+            if(value instanceof MeetingAccess.Locked held){access.requirePrepared(held,actor,s.subject().id(),affected,MeetingAccess.Action.EDIT);return true;}
+            if(value instanceof MeetingAccess.LockedIdentities identities){
+                var held=access.lockPrepared(identities,s.subject().id(),skip);
+                if(held.isEmpty())return false;
+                access.require(held.orElseThrow(),MeetingAccess.Action.EDIT);return true;
+            }
+            if(!"FACT_ONLY".equals(value))throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown proof");
+            return !j.queryForList("SELECT id FROM meeting WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE"+(skip?" SKIP LOCKED":""),String.class,s.subject().id()).isEmpty();
+        }
+        @Override public void validateInput(TaskSpec s){
+            var rows=j.queryForList("SELECT meeting_id,revision FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=?",s.input().id());
+            if(!s.subject().equals(spec.subject())||!s.input().equals(spec.input())||s.businessVersion()!=0||s.cancelEpoch()!=0||rows.size()!=1||!rows.getFirst().get("meeting_id").equals(meeting)
+                    ||((Number)rows.getFirst().get("revision")).longValue()!=s.businessVersion()
+                    ||!s.inputFingerprint().equals(Task08InheritedFixture.hash(input)))throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid seam input");
+        }
+        @Override public void validateOutput(TaskSpec s,TaskResult r){
+            if(r.output()!=null&&!r.output().equals(s.input()))throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid seam output");
+        }
+        @Override public void validateExternalRequest(TaskSpec s,ExternalRequest r){
+            if(!r.operationKind().equals("SEAM_CALL")||!r.requestManifest().equals("seam-v1:"+s.input().id()))throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown controlled request");
+        }
+        @Override public void validateExternalObservation(TaskSpec s,ama.contract.ExternalContracts.ExternalObservation o){
+            if(o.outcome()==ama.contract.ExternalContracts.ExternalOutcome.SUCCEEDED&&!o.externalResultId().startsWith("SEAM_RESULT:"))throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown controlled observation");
+            if(o.outcome()==ama.contract.ExternalContracts.ExternalOutcome.FAILED_ZERO_EFFECT&&!o.noEffectProof().safeEvidenceReference().equals("SEAM_VERIFIED_ZERO"))throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown controlled proof");
+        }
+    };
+    final JdbcOutbox outbox=new JdbcOutbox(source,Map.of("TEST_INFO",event->{
+        var rows=j.queryForList("SELECT meeting_id,revision FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=?",event.reference().id());
+        if(rows.size()!=1||!rows.getFirst().get("meeting_id").equals(event.subject().id())
+                ||((Number)rows.getFirst().get("revision")).longValue()!=event.subjectRevision())
+            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid seam event provenance");
+    }));
+    final JdbcTaskQueue queue=new JdbcTaskQueue(source,fences,outbox,Map.of(taskType,policy));
+

+        Task08InheritedResources.requireForward(source);
+        tx.required(()->{
+            j.update("INSERT INTO enterprise_identity(enterprise_id,display_name,status,verified_at) VALUES (?,?,'ACTIVE',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",owner,owner);
+            fences.create(spec.subject(),0,0);
+            j.update("INSERT INTO meeting(id,owner_id,stage,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'ENTRY',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",meeting,owner);
+            j.update("INSERT INTO meeting_info_revision(id,meeting_id,revision,name,created_by,created_at) VALUES (?,?,0,'seam',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",input,meeting,owner);
+            return null;
+        });
+        trace.clear();
+    }

+    @Test void standaloneClaimMustLockIdentityBeforeSubject(){
+        String taskId=queue.enqueue(spec);
+        trace.clear();
+        var lease=queue.claim(spec.pool(),"SEAM_WORKER");
+        assertTrue(lease.isPresent(),"real task must reach a real claim before lock-order assertion");
+        assertEquals(taskId,lease.orElseThrow().taskId());
+        List<String> sql=List.copyOf(trace);
+        System.out.println("B06 standalone claim actual locking SQL="+sql);
+        int identity=index(sql,"FROM enterprise_identity"),subject=index(sql,"FROM task_subject"),
+                root=index(sql,"SELECT * FROM meeting WHERE"),task=index(sql,"FROM durable_task WHERE id");
+        assertTrue(identity>=0&&subject>=0&&root>=0&&task>=0,"all real identity/subject/root/task locks must occur");
+        assertTrue(identity<subject&&subject<root&&root<task,
+                "canonical identity -> subject -> meeting -> task required; actual indexes="+List.of(identity,subject,root,task));
+    }

+    @Test void ambientEnqueueMustReuseAuthenticMeetingCapability(){
+        Throwable failure=null;
+        try{
+            tx.required(()->{
+                var held=access.lock(actor,meeting);
+                access.require(held,MeetingAccess.Action.EDIT);
+                queue.enqueue(spec,policy.adopt(spec,held));
+                outbox.flush();
+                return null;
+            });
+        }catch(RuntimeException observed){failure=observed;}
+        int tasks=j.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM durable_task WHERE producer_key=?",Integer.class,spec.producerKey());
+        int events=j.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM task_outbox WHERE subject_id=?",Integer.class,meeting);
+        System.out.println("B06 ambient actual="+(failure==null?"success":failure.getClass().getSimpleName()+": "+failure.getMessage())
+                +"; persisted tasks="+tasks+"; persisted events="+events+"; locking SQL="+trace);
+        assertNull(failure,"authenticated ambient enqueue must reuse the already-held B03 capability without a repeated identity phase");
+        assertEquals(1,tasks);
+        assertEquals(1,events);
+    }

+    @Test void disabledIdentityActuallyBlocksCompletionAndPreventsEffects()throws Exception{
+        queue.enqueue(spec);
+        TaskLease lease=queue.claim(spec.pool(),"SEAM_DISABLE_WORKER").orElseThrow();
+        var disabledHeld=new CountDownLatch(1);
+        var releaseDisabled=new CountDownLatch(1);
+        completionAtIdentity=new CountDownLatch(1);
+        var effects=new AtomicInteger();
+        trace.clear();
+        try(var threads=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2)){
+            var disable=threads.submit(()->tx.required(()->{
+                j.update("UPDATE enterprise_identity SET status='DISABLED' WHERE enterprise_id=?",owner);
+                disabledHeld.countDown();
+                await(releaseDisabled);
+                return true;
+            }));
+            try{
+                assertTrue(disabledHeld.await(10,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"disable transaction must hold real identity");
+                var finish=threads.submit(()->{
+                    completer.set(Thread.currentThread());
+                    return queue.completeWithEffects(lease,new TaskResult(Outcome.SUCCEEDED,spec.input(),null),()->{
+                        effects.incrementAndGet();
+                        j.update("UPDATE meeting SET stage='TRANSCRIPT_PENDING' WHERE id=?",meeting);
+                    });
+                });
+                assertTrue(completionAtIdentity.await(10,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"completion must reach actual identity SELECT FOR UPDATE");
+                assertFalse(finish.isDone(),"completion must wait while disable transaction owns identity");
+                releaseDisabled.countDown();
+                assertTrue(disable.get(10,TimeUnit.SECONDS));
+                assertInstanceOf(MeetingAccess.AccessDeniedException.class,
+                        assertThrows(ExecutionException.class,()->finish.get(10,TimeUnit.SECONDS)).getCause());
+            }finally{releaseDisabled.countDown();}
+        }
+        assertEquals(0,effects.get(),"disabled owner must have no completion effects");
+        assertEquals("ENTRY",j.queryForObject("SELECT stage FROM meeting WHERE id=?",String.class,meeting));
+        assertEquals("RUNNING",j.queryForObject("SELECT state FROM durable_task WHERE id=?",String.class,lease.taskId()));
+        assertNull(j.queryForObject("SELECT output_id FROM durable_task WHERE id=?",String.class,lease.taskId()));
+        System.out.println("B06 disable proof: stale Actor.status="+actor.status()+"; current shared identity=DISABLED; effects=0; task=RUNNING; actual locking SQL="+trace);
+    }
+    @Test void currentThreeArgumentCompletionValidatesOutputBeforeEffects(){
+        String task=queue.enqueue(spec);
+        TaskLease lease=queue.claim(spec.pool(),"SEAM_OUTPUT_WORKER").orElseThrow();
+        String output=UUID.randomUUID().toString();
+        var effects=new AtomicInteger();
+        var outputPolicy=new TaskDomainPolicy(){
+            public Optional<PreparedDomain> prepare(List<TaskSpec> specs,Acquisition purpose,boolean skip){return policy.prepare(specs,purpose,skip);}
+            public void requirePrepared(List<TaskSpec> specs,Acquisition purpose,PreparedDomain held){policy.requirePrepared(specs,purpose,held);}
+            public boolean permitsDomainEffects(PreparedDomain held){return policy.permitsDomainEffects(held);}
+            public boolean lockRoot(TaskSpec s,boolean skip,PreparedDomain held){return policy.lockRoot(s,skip,held);}
+            public boolean lockRoot(TaskSpec s,boolean skip){return policy.lockRoot(s,skip);}
+            public void validateInput(TaskSpec s){policy.validateInput(s);}
+            public void validateOutput(TaskSpec s,TaskResult r){
+                var rows=j.queryForList("SELECT meeting_id,revision FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=?",r.output().id());
+                if(!r.output().kind().equals("TEST_INFO")||rows.size()!=1
+                        ||!rows.getFirst().get("meeting_id").equals(meeting)
+                        ||((Number)rows.getFirst().get("revision")).longValue()!=1)
+                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Output row not present before effects");
+            }
+            public void validateExternalRequest(TaskSpec s,ExternalRequest r){policy.validateExternalRequest(s,r);}
+        };
+        var outputQueue=new JdbcTaskQueue(source,fences,outbox,Map.of(taskType,outputPolicy));
+        var failure=assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,()->outputQueue.completeWithEffects(lease,
+                new TaskResult(Outcome.SUCCEEDED,new TypedRef("TEST_INFO",output),null),()->{
+                    effects.incrementAndGet();
+                    j.update("INSERT INTO meeting_info_revision(id,meeting_id,revision,name,created_by,created_at) VALUES (?,?,1,'output',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",output,meeting,owner);
+                }));
+        assertEquals("Output row not present before effects",failure.getMessage());
+        assertEquals(0,effects.get());
+        assertEquals(0,j.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=?",Integer.class,output));
+        assertEquals("RUNNING",j.queryForObject("SELECT state FROM durable_task WHERE id=?",String.class,task));
+        System.out.println("B06 existing three-argument output ordering control: validation rejected absent real output before effects; effects=0; output rows=0; task=RUNNING. This is existing API behavior, not a production defect.");
+    }
+    @Test void completeIdentityUnionUsesBinaryOrderBeforeAnySubject(){
+        String prefix="t08-b06-union-"+UUID.randomUUID()+"-",bmp=prefix+"\uE000",nonBmp=prefix+"\uD800\uDC00";
+        for(String id:List.of(bmp,nonBmp))j.update("INSERT INTO enterprise_identity(enterprise_id,display_name,status,verified_at) VALUES (?,?,'ACTIVE',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",id,id);
+        policy.affected.addAll(List.of(nonBmp,bmp));trace.clear();
+        queue.enqueue(spec);
+        var identities=trace.stream().filter(x->x.contains("FROM enterprise_identity")).toList();
+        assertEquals(3,identities.size());
+        var expected=new ArrayList<>(List.of(owner,bmp,nonBmp));
+        expected.sort((a,b)->Arrays.compareUnsigned(a.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8),b.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
+        for(int i=0;i<expected.size();i++)assertTrue(identities.get(i).endsWith("/*identity="+expected.get(i)+"*/"),identities.toString());
+        int firstSubject=index(trace,"FROM task_subject");
+        for(String sql:identities)assertTrue(trace.indexOf(sql)<firstSubject);
+        assertEquals(expected,j.queryForList("SELECT enterprise_id FROM enterprise_identity WHERE enterprise_id IN (?,?,?) ORDER BY enterprise_id COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin",String.class,owner,bmp,nonBmp));
+    }

+                });

+            assertTrue(blocker.get(10,TimeUnit.SECONDS));
+        }

+    static void await(CountDownLatch latch){try{if(!latch.await(10,TimeUnit.SECONDS))throw new AssertionError("JDBC gate timeout");}catch(InterruptedException e){Thread.currentThread().interrupt();throw new AssertionError(e);}}
+    interface Probe {void before(String sql);}
+    static final class ProbeSource extends AbstractDataSource {
+        final DataSource delegate;final Probe probe;
+        ProbeSource(DataSource delegate,Probe probe){this.delegate=delegate;this.probe=probe;}
+        @Override public Connection getConnection()throws SQLException{
+            var real=delegate.getConnection();
+            real.setTransactionIsolation(Connection.TRANSACTION_REPEATABLE_READ);
+            return (Connection)Proxy.newProxyInstance(getClass().getClassLoader(),new Class<?>[]{Connection.class},(p,m,a)->{
+                Object value=invoke(real,m,a);
+                if(m.getName().equals("prepareStatement")){
+                    String sql=(String)a[0];PreparedStatement statement=(PreparedStatement)value;var parameters=new HashMap<Integer,Object>();
+                    statement.setQueryTimeout(10);
+                    return Proxy.newProxyInstance(getClass().getClassLoader(),new Class<?>[]{PreparedStatement.class},(p2,m2,a2)->{
+                        if(m2.getName().startsWith("set")&&a2!=null&&a2.length>=2&&a2[0] instanceof Integer n)parameters.put(n,a2[1]);
+                        if(m2.getName().startsWith("execute"))probe.before(sql+(sql.contains("FROM enterprise_identity")?" /*identity="+parameters.get(1)+"*/":""));
+                        return invoke(statement,m2,a2);
+                    });
+                }
+                return value;
+            });
+        }
+        @Override public Connection getConnection(String u,String p)throws SQLException{return getConnection();}
+        static Object invoke(Object target,Method method,Object[] args)throws Throwable{try{return method.invoke(target,args);}catch(InvocationTargetException e){throw e.getCause();}}
+    }

+package ama.task;
+
+import ama.contract.OutboxContracts.*;
+import ama.contract.TaskContracts.SubjectRef;
+import ama.db.TransactionRunner;
+import ama.support.MysqlTestDatabase;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.Set;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import javax.sql.DataSource;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
+import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization;
+import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;
+import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
+
+/** Prepared for the centrally frozen V010. Never migrates, resets, or repairs an existing fixture. */
+class Task08InheritedOutboxFenceIT {
+    DataSource source;
+    JdbcTemplate jdbc;
+    TransactionRunner tx;
+    JdbcSubjectFences fences;
+    JdbcOutbox outbox;
+    SubjectRef first;
+    SubjectRef second;
+
+    @BeforeEach void prepareDataAfterApprovedMigration() {
+        source = Task08InheritedResources.source();
+        jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(source);
+        assertEquals(1, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM flyway_schema_history WHERE version='010' AND success=1", Integer.class),
+                "Requires a new owned fixture with centrally frozen, ordered V010 already applied");
+        tx = new TransactionRunner(source);
+        fences = new JdbcSubjectFences(source, Set.of("MEETING"));
+        outbox = new JdbcOutbox(source, Map.of("MEETING", event -> {
+            if (!event.subject().kind().equals("MEETING") || !event.subject().id().equals(event.reference().id())) {
+                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Cross-subject reference");
+            }
+            Long version = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT revision FROM meeting WHERE id=?", Long.class, event.reference().id());
+            if (version != event.subjectRevision()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Wrong event version");
+        }));
+        first = createMeeting(); second = createMeeting();
+    }
+
+    SubjectRef createMeeting() {
+        var subject = new SubjectRef("MEETING", UUID.randomUUID().toString());
+        String owner = "t08-inherited-test-" + UUID.randomUUID();
+        tx.required(() -> {
+            jdbc.update("INSERT INTO enterprise_identity(enterprise_id,display_name,status,session_generation,verified_at) VALUES (?,?,'ACTIVE',0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", owner, owner);
+            fences.create(subject, 0, 0);
+            jdbc.update("INSERT INTO meeting(id,owner_id,stage,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'ENTRY',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", subject.id(), owner);
+            return null;
+        });
+        return subject;
+    }
+
+    EventDraft event(SubjectRef subject) {
+        return new EventDraft("MEETING_CHANGED", null, subject, 0, new Reference("MEETING", subject.id()));
+    }
+
+    long cursor() { return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT next_id-1 FROM task_outbox_sequence WHERE id=1", Long.class); }
+
+    @Test void delayedLowerCommitCannotBeSkippedByCursorReader() throws Exception {
+        long baseline = cursor();
+        var firstFlushed = new CountDownLatch(1);
+        var releaseFirst = new CountDownLatch(1);
+        var secondReady = new CountDownLatch(1);
+        try (var workers = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2)) {
+            var a = workers.submit(() -> tx.required(() -> {
+                fences.lock(first, false).orElseThrow();
+                jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT revision FROM meeting WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE", Long.class, first.id());
+                outbox.append(event(first)); outbox.flush(); firstFlushed.countDown();
+                if (!releaseFirst.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) throw new IllegalStateException("Test release timed out");
+                return null;
+            }));
+            assertTrue(firstFlushed.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS));
+            var b = workers.submit(() -> tx.required(() -> {
+                fences.lock(second, false).orElseThrow();
+                jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT revision FROM meeting WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE", Long.class, second.id());
+                outbox.append(event(second)); secondReady.countDown(); outbox.flush();
+                return null;
+            }));
+            try {
+                assertTrue(secondReady.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS));
+                assertThrows(TimeoutException.class, () -> b.get(250, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS));
+                assertTrue(outbox.eventsAfter(baseline, 10).isEmpty());
+            } finally { releaseFirst.countDown(); }
+            a.get(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS); b.get(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
+        }
+        var delivered = outbox.eventsAfter(baseline, 10);
+        assertEquals(2, delivered.size());
+        assertEquals(baseline + 1, delivered.getFirst().sequence());
+        assertEquals(first, delivered.getFirst().event().subject());
+        assertEquals(baseline + 2, delivered.getLast().sequence());
+        assertEquals(second, delivered.getLast().event().subject());
+    }
+
+    @Test void rollbackReturnsSequenceAndLeavesNoEventOrDomainEffect() {
+        long baseline = cursor();
+        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> {
+            var held = fences.lock(first, false).orElseThrow();
+            jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT revision FROM meeting WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE", Long.class, first.id());
+            fences.advance(held, 1, 0, false);
+            jdbc.update("UPDATE meeting SET revision=1 WHERE id=?", first.id());
+            outbox.append(new EventDraft("MEETING_CHANGED", null, first, 1, new Reference("MEETING", first.id())));
+            outbox.flush();
+            throw new IllegalStateException("Rollback after the final flush");
+        }));
+        assertEquals(baseline, cursor());
+        assertTrue(outbox.eventsAfter(baseline, 10).isEmpty());
+        assertEquals(0, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT revision FROM meeting WHERE id=?", Long.class, first.id()));
+        tx.required(() -> { outbox.append(event(first)); outbox.flush(); return null; });
+        assertEquals(baseline + 1, outbox.eventsAfter(baseline, 1).getFirst().sequence());
+    }
+
+    @Test void missingFlushAndForeignCommitCallbacksAreRejectedWithoutEffects() {
+        long baseline = cursor();
+        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> { outbox.append(event(first)); return null; }));
+        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> {
+            outbox.append(event(first));
+            TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {});
+            outbox.flush(); return null;
+        }));
+        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> {
+            outbox.append(event(first)); outbox.flush();
+            TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
+                @Override public void beforeCommit(boolean readOnly) {
+                    jdbc.update("UPDATE meeting SET revision=99 WHERE id=?", first.id());
+                }
+            });
+            return null;
+        }));
+        assertEquals(baseline, cursor());
+        assertEquals(0, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT revision FROM meeting WHERE id=?", Long.class, first.id()));
+        tx.required(() -> { outbox.append(event(first)); outbox.flush(); return null; });
+        assertEquals(1, outbox.eventsAfter(baseline, 10).size(), "Rollback must release transaction-scoped buffer");
+    }
+
+    @Test void tombstoneCannotBeReinitializedOrRevived() {
+        tx.required(() -> {
+            var held = fences.lock(first, false).orElseThrow();
+            jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT revision FROM meeting WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE", Long.class, first.id());
+            fences.advance(held, 1, 1, true);
+            jdbc.update("UPDATE meeting SET revision=1,cancel_epoch=1,lifecycle='DELETED' WHERE id=?", first.id());
+            return null;
+        });
+        assertThrows(RuntimeException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> { fences.create(first, 0, 0); return null; }));
+        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> {
+            fences.advance(fences.lock(first, false).orElseThrow(), 2, 2, false); return null;
+        }));
+        tx.required(() -> { assertTrue(fences.lock(first, false).orElseThrow().deleted()); return null; });
+    }
+
+    @Test void missingSubjectAndUnregisteredReferencesNeverCreateRows() {
+        var missing = new SubjectRef("MEETING", UUID.randomUUID().toString());
+        tx.required(() -> {
+            assertTrue(fences.lock(missing, true).isEmpty());
+            assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> outbox.append(new EventDraft("MEETING_CHANGED", null, first, 0,
+                    new Reference("UNREGISTERED", first.id()))));
+            return null;
+        });
+        assertEquals(0, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM task_subject WHERE subject_id=?", Integer.class, missing.id()));
+    }
+
+    @Test void domainLocksAndNewEventsAreRejectedAfterFinalFlush() {
+        long baseline = cursor();
+        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> {
+            outbox.append(event(first)); outbox.flush(); fences.lock(second, false); return null;
+        }));
+        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> {
+            outbox.append(event(first)); outbox.flush(); outbox.append(event(second)); return null;
+        }));
+        assertEquals(baseline, cursor());
+    }
+
+    @Test void concurrentGlobalProducersCommitOneContinuousPrefix() throws Exception {
+        var subjects = new java.util.ArrayList<SubjectRef>();
+        for (int n=0;n<20;n++) subjects.add(createMeeting());
+        long baseline=cursor();
+        try(var workers=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(8)) {
+            var futures=new java.util.ArrayList<Future<?>>();
+            for(var subject:subjects) futures.add(workers.submit(()->tx.required(()->{
+                fences.lock(subject,false).orElseThrow();
+                jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT id FROM meeting WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",String.class,subject.id());
+                outbox.append(event(subject));outbox.flush();return null;
+            })));
+            for(var future:futures) future.get(15,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
+        }
+        var events=outbox.eventsAfter(baseline,30);assertEquals(20,events.size());
+        for(int n=0;n<20;n++)assertEquals(baseline+n+1,events.get(n).sequence());
+        assertEquals(20,events.stream().map(e->e.event().subject()).distinct().count());
+    }
+}

+package ama.task;
+
+import ama.access.MeetingAccess;
+import ama.contract.TaskContracts.*;
+import ama.contract.ExternalContracts.ExternalRequest;
+import ama.contract.IdentityContracts.*;
+import ama.db.*;
+import ama.meeting.JdbcMeetingAccess;
+import ama.support.MysqlTestDatabase;
+import ama.worker.WorkerLoop;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.atomic.*;
+import java.util.function.Supplier;
+import javax.sql.DataSource;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
+import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;
+import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
+
+/** Real owned MySQL, authentic B03 identity preparation and actual immutable output rows. No resets. */
+class Task08InheritedPreparedCompletionIT {
+    final AtomicBoolean failFlush = new AtomicBoolean(), failAudit = new AtomicBoolean(), failEvent = new AtomicBoolean();
+    final DataSource source = new Task08InheritedB06QueueSeamsIT.ProbeSource(Task08InheritedResources.source(), sql -> {
+        if (failAudit.get() && sql.startsWith("INSERT INTO audit_event(")) throw new IllegalStateException("CONTROLLED_AUDIT_FAILURE");
+        if (failFlush.get() && sql.startsWith("INSERT INTO task_outbox(")) throw new IllegalStateException("CONTROLLED_FLUSH_FAILURE");
+    });
+    final JdbcTemplate jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(source);
+    final TransactionRunner tx = new TransactionRunner(source);
+    final JdbcSubjectFences fences = new JdbcSubjectFences(source, Set.of("MEETING"));
+    final JdbcMeetingAccess access = new JdbcMeetingAccess(source, tx, fences);
+    final String owner = "prepared-" + UUID.randomUUID(), meeting = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
+    final String input = UUID.randomUUID().toString(), output = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
+    final Actor actor = new Actor(owner, Status.ACTIVE, Set.of(Role.USER));
+    final TaskSpec spec = new TaskSpec("PREPARED_TEST", "PREPARED_" + UUID.randomUUID(),
+            new SubjectRef("MEETING", meeting), Task08InheritedFixture.hash(input + "producer"),
+            new TypedRef("TEST_INFO", input), Task08InheritedFixture.hash(input), 0, 0, 0, 3);
+    final AtomicInteger writes = new AtomicInteger(), validations = new AtomicInteger();
+    final TaskDomainPolicy policy = new TaskDomainPolicy() {
+        final JdbcTaskPreparations preparations = new JdbcTaskPreparations(source);
+        public Optional<PreparedDomain> prepare(List<TaskSpec> specs, Acquisition purpose, boolean skip) {
+            return access.tryLockIdentities(actor, List.of(), skip)
+                    .map(held -> preparations.issue(specs, purpose, held, true));
+        }
+        public void requirePrepared(List<TaskSpec> specs, Acquisition purpose, PreparedDomain held) { preparations.require(specs, purpose, held); }
+        public boolean permitsDomainEffects(PreparedDomain held) { return preparations.permitsEffects(held); }
+        public boolean lockRoot(TaskSpec s, boolean skip) { throw new IllegalStateException("Authentic preparation required"); }
+        public boolean lockRoot(TaskSpec s, boolean skip, PreparedDomain prepared) {
+            var identities = (MeetingAccess.LockedIdentities) preparations.value(prepared);
+            var held = access.lockPrepared(identities, meeting, skip);
+            if (held.isEmpty()) return false;
+            access.require(held.orElseThrow(), MeetingAccess.Action.EDIT);
+            return !jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE" + (skip ? " SKIP LOCKED" : ""), String.class, input).isEmpty();
+        }
+        public void validateInput(TaskSpec s) {
+            if (!s.equals(spec) || jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=? AND meeting_id=? AND revision=0 AND name='input'", Integer.class, input, meeting) != 1)
+                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid saved input");
+        }
+        public void validateOutput(TaskSpec s, TaskResult result) {
+            validations.incrementAndGet();
+            if (result.output() == null || !result.output().equals(new TypedRef("TEST_INFO", output))
+                    || jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=? AND meeting_id=? AND revision=1 AND name='output'", Integer.class, output, meeting) != 1)
+                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Actual output must exist with matching provenance");
+            assertEquals("RUNNING", state(), "terminal mutation must follow actual output validation");
+        }
+        public void validateExternalRequest(TaskSpec s, ExternalRequest request) { throw new IllegalArgumentException("No external authority"); }
+    };
+    final JdbcOutbox outbox = new JdbcOutbox(source, Map.of("TEST_INFO", event -> {
+        if (failEvent.get()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("CONTROLLED_EVENT_FAILURE");
+        if (!event.subject().equals(spec.subject()) || !event.reference().id().equals(input)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid event provenance");
+    }));
+    final JdbcTaskQueue queue = new JdbcTaskQueue(source, fences, outbox, Map.of(spec.type(), policy));
+    TaskLease lease;
+
+    @BeforeAll static void migrateOwnedEmptyFixture() {
+        var source = Task08InheritedResources.source();
+        Task08InheritedResources.requireForward(source);
+        var history = new JdbcTemplate(source).queryForList("SELECT installed_rank,version,script,checksum,success FROM flyway_schema_history ORDER BY installed_rank");
+        assertEquals(15, history.size());
+        System.out.println("PREPARED completion pre-test immutable history: " + history);
+    }
+    @BeforeEach void setup() {
+        tx.required(() -> {
+            jdbc.update("INSERT INTO enterprise_identity(enterprise_id,display_name,status,verified_at) VALUES (?,?,'ACTIVE',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", owner, owner);
+            fences.create(spec.subject(), 0, 0);
+            jdbc.update("INSERT INTO meeting(id,owner_id,stage,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'ENTRY',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", meeting, owner);
+            jdbc.update("INSERT INTO meeting_info_revision(id,meeting_id,revision,name,created_by,created_at) VALUES (?,?,0,'input',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", input, meeting, owner);
+            return null;
+        });
+        queue.enqueue(spec);
+        lease = queue.claim(spec.pool(), "PREPARED_WORKER").orElseThrow();
+    }
+    TaskResult writeOutput() {
+        assertTrue(TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive());
+        writes.incrementAndGet();
+        jdbc.update("INSERT INTO meeting_info_revision(id,meeting_id,revision,name,created_by,created_at) VALUES (?,?,1,'output',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", output, meeting, owner);
+        new ama.audit.JdbcAuditAdapter(source).append(new ama.contract.AuditContracts.AuditEvent(
+                owner, "PREPARED_OUTPUT", meeting, 1, "SUCCESS", UUID.randomUUID().toString(), java.time.Instant.now()));
+        return new TaskResult(Outcome.SUCCEEDED, new TypedRef("TEST_INFO", output), null);
+    }
+    String state() { return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT state FROM durable_task WHERE producer_key=?", String.class, spec.producerKey()); }
+    int events() { return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM task_outbox WHERE task_id=?", Integer.class, lease.taskId()); }
+    void rolledBack() {
+        assertEquals(0, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=?", Integer.class, output));
+        assertEquals("RUNNING", state());
+        assertNull(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT output_id FROM durable_task WHERE id=?", String.class, lease.taskId()));
+        assertNull(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT ended_at FROM task_attempt WHERE task_id=? AND attempt=1", java.sql.Timestamp.class, lease.taskId()));
+        assertEquals(1, events());
+        assertEquals(0, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audit_event WHERE resource_id=?", Integer.class, meeting));
+    }
+    boolean complete(Supplier<TaskResult> effects) { return queue.completeWithEffects(lease, effects); }
+    @Test void newOutputIsWrittenBeforeValidationAndCommitsWithTaskAndAttempt() {
+        assertEquals(0, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=?", Integer.class, output));
+        assertTrue(assertDoesNotThrow(() -> complete(this::writeOutput)));
+        assertEquals(1, writes.get()); assertEquals(1, validations.get());
+        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", state());
+        assertEquals(output, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT output_id FROM durable_task WHERE id=?", String.class, lease.taskId()));
+        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT outcome FROM task_attempt WHERE task_id=? AND attempt=1", String.class, lease.taskId()));
+        assertEquals(1, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM meeting_info_revision WHERE id=?", Integer.class, output));
+        assertEquals(2, events());
+        assertEquals(1, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audit_event WHERE resource_id=?", Integer.class, meeting));
+        assertFalse(complete(() -> { fail("duplicate completion wrote output"); return null; }));
+    }
+    @Test void invalidOutputRollsBackItsRowAndTaskAttemptAndOutbox() {
+        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> complete(() -> { writeOutput(); return new TaskResult(Outcome.SUCCEEDED, new TypedRef("OTHER_KIND", output), null); }));
+        assertEquals(1, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void callbackThrowRollsBackItsRowAndTaskAttemptAndOutbox() {
+        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> complete(() -> { writeOutput(); throw new IllegalStateException("CONTROLLED_CALLBACK_FAILURE"); }));
+        assertEquals(1, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void nullResultRollsBackItsRowAndTaskAttemptAndOutbox() {
+        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> complete(() -> { writeOutput(); return null; }));
+        assertEquals(1, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void flushFailureRollsBackOutputTaskAttemptAndOutbox() {

+    }

+        assertFalse(complete(this::writeOutput)); assertEquals(0, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void staleVersionSuppressesCallback() {

+        assertFalse(complete(this::writeOutput)); assertEquals(0, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }

+        assertThrows(MeetingAccess.AccessDeniedException.class, () -> complete(this::writeOutput));

+    }

+        assertEquals(0, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void oldRunnableContractStillValidatesBeforeWriting() {

+        assertEquals(0, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }

+    }

+        failEvent.set(true);

+    }
+    @Test void cancelledEpochSuppressesCallback() {

+        assertFalse(complete(this::writeOutput)); assertEquals(0, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void forgedOwnerAndAttemptSuppressCallback() {
+        var wrongOwner = new TaskLease(lease.taskId(), lease.attempt(), "FOREIGN_WORKER", lease.expiresAt(), spec);
+        var wrongAttempt = new TaskLease(lease.taskId(), lease.attempt() + 1, lease.leaseOwner(), lease.expiresAt(), spec);
+        assertFalse(queue.completeWithEffects(wrongOwner, this::writeOutput));
+        assertFalse(queue.completeWithEffects(wrongAttempt, this::writeOutput));
+        assertEquals(0, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void nullCallbackIsRejectedWithoutAnyMutation() {
+        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> queue.completeWithEffects(lease, (Supplier<TaskResult>) null));
+        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> queue.completeWithEffects(null, this::writeOutput));
+        assertEquals(0, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void callbackMayAdvanceItsPrelockedDomainBeforeTerminalMutation() {
+        assertTrue(complete(() -> {
+            var result = writeOutput();
+            jdbc.update("UPDATE meeting SET revision=1 WHERE id=?", meeting);
+            jdbc.update("UPDATE task_subject SET business_version=1 WHERE subject_kind='MEETING' AND subject_id=?", meeting);
+            return result;
+        }));
+        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", state());
+        assertEquals(1L, jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT business_version FROM task_subject WHERE subject_kind='MEETING' AND subject_id=?", Long.class, meeting));
+    }
+    @Test void ambientTransactionOwnsFinalFlushAndRollback() {
+        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> tx.required(() -> {
+            assertTrue(complete(this::writeOutput));
+            assertEquals(1, events(), "queue must not flush the caller's transaction early");
+            outbox.flush();
+            assertEquals(2, events());
+            throw new IllegalStateException("CALLER_ABORT");
+        }));
+        assertEquals(1, writes.get()); rolledBack();
+    }
+    @Test void actualWorkerPreparesOutsideTransactionAndCommitsExactlyOnce() throws Exception {
+        jdbc.update("UPDATE durable_task SET expires_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)-INTERVAL 1 SECOND WHERE id=?", lease.taskId());
+        var done = new CountDownLatch(1); var preparedCalls = new AtomicInteger(); var ordinaryCalls = new AtomicInteger();
+        TaskQueue observed = new TaskQueue() {
+            public String enqueue(TaskSpec s) { return queue.enqueue(s); }
+            public Optional<TaskLease> claim(String pool, String owner) { return queue.claim(pool, owner); }
+            public boolean renew(TaskLease l) { return queue.renew(l); }
+            public boolean complete(TaskLease l, TaskResult result) { ordinaryCalls.incrementAndGet(); try { return queue.complete(l, result); } finally { done.countDown(); } }
+            public boolean completeWithEffects(TaskLease l, TaskResult result, Runnable effects) { throw new AssertionError("Wrong API"); }
+            public boolean completeWithEffects(TaskLease l, Supplier<TaskResult> effects) {
+                preparedCalls.incrementAndGet(); try { return queue.completeWithEffects(l, effects); } finally { done.countDown(); }
+            }
+        };
+        var preparationOutside = new AtomicBoolean();
+        WorkerLoop.PreparedHandler handler = l -> {
+            preparationOutside.set(!TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive());
+            assertEquals(0, writes.get());
+            return this::writeOutput;
+        };
+        try (var worker = WorkerLoop.prepared(observed, Map.of(spec.pool(), 1), Map.of(spec.type(), handler), "ACTUAL_WORKER")) {
+            worker.pollOnce(spec.pool());
+            assertTrue(done.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS));
+        }
+        assertTrue(preparationOutside.get()); assertEquals(1, preparedCalls.get()); assertEquals(0, ordinaryCalls.get());
+        assertEquals(1, writes.get()); assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", state()); assertEquals(2, events());
+        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT outcome FROM task_attempt WHERE task_id=? AND attempt=2", String.class, lease.taskId()));
+    }

+package ama.task;
+import ama.contract.ExternalContracts.OperationToken;
+import java.util.*;
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
+import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

+package ama.task;
+
+import java.lang.reflect.*;
+import java.sql.*;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;
+import java.util.function.Consumer;
+import javax.sql.DataSource;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.datasource.AbstractDataSource;
+
+/** Actual JDBC observation and deterministic fault gates; no fake database answers. */
+final class Task08ProbeSource extends AbstractDataSource {
+ record Sql(String text,Map<Integer,Object> parameters) {}
+ final DataSource delegate;
+ final List<Sql> sql=new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
+ final List<String> controls=new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
+ volatile long rows;
+ volatile Consumer<Sql> before=x->{},after=x->{};
+ volatile Runnable committed=()->{};
+ Task08ProbeSource(DataSource delegate){this.delegate=delegate;}
+ void clear(){sql.clear();controls.clear();rows=0;}
+ public Connection getConnection()throws SQLException{
+  Connection real=delegate.getConnection();controls.add("acquire");
+  return (Connection)Proxy.newProxyInstance(getClass().getClassLoader(),new Class<?>[]{Connection.class},(p,m,a)->{
+   String name=m.getName();
+   if(Set.of("setAutoCommit","commit","rollback","close","setReadOnly","setTransactionIsolation","setSavepoint","releaseSavepoint").contains(name))controls.add(name+(a==null?"":Arrays.toString(a)));
+   Object value=invoke(real,m,a);
+   if(name.equals("commit"))committed.run();
+   if(value instanceof Statement statement && (name.equals("prepareStatement")||name.equals("createStatement"))){
+    String prepared=name.equals("prepareStatement")?(String)a[0]:null;Map<Integer,Object> params=new TreeMap<>();
+    return Proxy.newProxyInstance(getClass().getClassLoader(),new Class<?>[]{prepared==null?Statement.class:PreparedStatement.class},(p2,m2,a2)->{
+     String n=m2.getName();
+     if(prepared!=null&&n.startsWith("set")&&a2!=null&&a2.length>=2&&a2[0] instanceof Integer k)params.put(k,a2[1]);
+     Sql event=null;if(n.startsWith("execute")){event=new Sql(prepared==null?(String)a2[0]:prepared,Collections.unmodifiableMap(new TreeMap<>(params)));sql.add(event);before.accept(event);}
+     Object v=invoke(statement,m2,a2);
+     if(event!=null){if(v instanceof Integer count&&count>0)rows+=count;if(v instanceof Long count&&count>0)rows+=count;after.accept(event);}
+     if(v instanceof ResultSet result)return Proxy.newProxyInstance(getClass().getClassLoader(),new Class<?>[]{ResultSet.class},(p3,m3,a3)->{Object x=invoke(result,m3,a3);if(m3.getName().equals("next")&&Boolean.TRUE.equals(x))rows++;return x;});
+     return v;
+    });
+   }
+   return value;
+  });
+ }
+ public Connection getConnection(String u,String p)throws SQLException{return getConnection();}
+ static Object invoke(Object target,Method m,Object[] a)throws Throwable{try{return m.invoke(target,a);}catch(InvocationTargetException e){throw e.getCause();}}
+}

+# AMA 数据字典 — B00-1.6-reconcile-scan
 
 生成源：`tools/checks/database_design.py`。与审阅 DDL 同步，实际 MySQL 元数据另行比对；生成本身不构成验证。
 
 全表 InnoDB / utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci；稳定标识使用 utf8mb4_0900_bin（NO PAD），UUID/协议码/哈希用 ascii_bin。每字段的类型中明确覆盖排序规则。DATETIME(6) 一律 UTC；DATE 为 Asia/Shanghai 业务日。默认值 `无` 意味必须由应用明确赋值（可空字段默认 NULL）。主键唯一性的业务依据为稳定实体标识或明确复合业务键；UUID 不承载顺序。LONGTEXT 的物理容量不是产品字数限制。
 
 全部 FK 均 ON UPDATE RESTRICT / ON DELETE RESTRICT，根与历史不级联；拥有任务负责在同事务验证同会议/同所有者、状态及快照成员关系。字典中无 FK 的 typed ID 和循环当前指针见设计文档“逻辑关系与删除”，不可按裸 ID 绕过鉴权。
 
 R1上下文根：所有纳入minutes_element.source_context_id无论证据数量均为basis根，与域内精确usage上下文取并集。ROWSET v2/PINNED用已保存输入要素集全部basis及set证据/目录，排除body/applied exclusion；REVIEW_SOURCES仅选定复核要素basis及精确相交证据（正文复核无要素basis）；FINAL用全部冻结纳入要素basis及既有body/set证据、目录、applied exclusion。各根完整context/selection/profile按实际完整PK去重及unsigned UTF8排序，缺失/额外行拒绝；无usage的basis同样验证并保留依赖。basis不伪造EVIDENCE、不增加usage数、不授予支持信用；草稿仍无自身fingerprint/TaskSpec，祖先按图依赖判断。

 ## file_cleanup
 
 可重试删除/孤儿清理登记。所属 B-04/D-07，计划 V009。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|内部 UUID；服务器生成，永不复用|
 |file_id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|已登记文件；孤儿为空|
 |backend_id|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|待删位置固定后端|
 |namespace|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|受控命名空间|
 |object_key|`varchar(512) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|受限枚举得到的逻辑键|
 |expected_location_revision|`bigint unsigned`|是|无|已登记文件的位置栅栏|
 |expected_reference_epoch|`bigint unsigned`|是|无|引用栅栏|
 |state|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|清理状态|
 |not_before|`datetime(6)`|否|无|配置恢复窗口后的可清理时间|
 |attempt|`bigint unsigned`|否|0|尝试次数|
 |last_error|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|安全错误码|
 
 主键：`id`。
 - 索引 `cleanup_due(state,not_before,id)`：清理候选分页。
 - 唯一约束 `cleanup_target(backend_id,namespace,object_key)`：位置仅一清理项。
 - CHECK `ck_file_cleanup_0`：`state IN ('PENDING','RUNNING','FAILED','COMPLETE')`。
 - FK `fk_file_cleanup_0`：`file_id` → `file_object(id)`。同一事务校验同会议/同所有者；删除 RESTRICT，先显式解除子引用。
 - FK `fk_file_cleanup_1`：`backend_id` → `storage_backend(id)`。同一事务校验同会议/同所有者；删除 RESTRICT，先显式解除子引用。
 
 更新/删除与完整性：孤儿不允许业务读取；清理前检查 upload/recording 进行中以及所有 file_reference；未配置恢复窗口不清理技术孤儿；不能自动清理历史成果。

 ## durable_task
 
 持久任务；外部未知结果从普通领取隔离。所属 B-05，计划 V010。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|内部 UUID；服务器生成，永不复用|

 |subject_id|`varchar(255) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin`|否|无|聚合ID|
 |producer_key|`char(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|稳定 enqueue 业务键摘要|
 |input_kind|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|输入快照类型|
 |input_id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|不可变输入快照ID|

 |business_version|`bigint unsigned`|否|无|入队版本|
 |cancel_epoch|`bigint unsigned`|否|无|入队取消代际|
 |priority|`int`|否|无|有界队列优先级|
 |state|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|'READY'|任务状态|
 |attempt|`bigint unsigned`|否|0|每次领取递增|
 |max_attempts|`bigint unsigned`|否|无|显式策略上限；含崩溃回收|
 |lease_owner|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|当前领取者|
 |expires_at|`datetime(6)`|是|无|DB租约截止|
 |available_at|`datetime(6)`|否|无|下次允许领取|
 |created_at|`datetime(6)`|否|无|入队时间|
 |output_kind|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|输出类型|
 |output_id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|输出ID|
 |last_error|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|安全失败码|
 
 主键：`id`。
 - 唯一约束 `task_producer(producer_key)`：同一业务入队重放唯一。
 - 索引 `task_ready(pool,state,available_at,id)`：分开扫描 READY。
 - 索引 `task_expired(pool,state,expires_at,id)`：分开扫描过期 RUNNING。
 - 索引 `task_subject_history(subject_kind,subject_id,created_at,id)`：聚合任务历史。

 - CHECK `ck_durable_task_1`：`max_attempts > 0`。
 - CHECK `ck_durable_task_2`：`state <> 'RUNNING' OR (lease_owner IS NOT NULL AND expires_at IS NOT NULL)`。
 - FK `fk_durable_task_0`：`subject_kind,subject_id` → `task_subject(subject_kind,subject_id)`。同一事务校验同会议/同所有者；删除 RESTRICT，先显式解除子引用。
 
 更新/删除与完整性：input/output typed refs 由任务注册器校验具体领域存在且相同 subject/版本；producer_key 冲突需比较完整输入，否则冲突；失败/旧结果保留最小诊断不覆盖有效稿。
 
 ## task_attempt
 
 每次 Worker 领取诊断历史。所属 B-05，计划 V010。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |task_id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|任务|
 |attempt|`bigint unsigned`|否|无|领取代际|
 |owner|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|Worker身份|
 |started_at|`datetime(6)`|否|无|开始|
 |ended_at|`datetime(6)`|是|无|结束|
 |outcome|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|结束结果|
 |error_code|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|安全错误码|
 
 主键：`task_id,attempt`。
 - FK `fk_task_attempt_0`：`task_id` → `durable_task(id)`。同一事务校验同会议/同所有者；删除 RESTRICT，先显式解除子引用。
 
 更新/删除与完整性：版本/快照 INSERT 后不可改写；可变状态仅由所属领域服务 CAS；历史无自动过期；会议删除按依赖逆序显式清理。
 
 ## external_operation
 
 任何可能产生外部效果的调用前 durable marker 及独立对账所有权。所属 B-05，计划 V010。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|内部 UUID；服务器生成，永不复用|
 |operation_kind|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|MODEL_GENERATE/PUBLISH/WITHDRAW/MAIL等|
 |business_key|`varchar(255) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin`|否|无|稳定外部幂等/查回键|
 |request_hash|`char(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|逻辑内容摘要：固定confirmation/body及业务操作；不替代每次完整请求摘要|
 |state|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|调用/对账状态|
 |marked_at|`datetime(6)`|否|无|首次before-external marker实际提交时间|
 |next_check_at|`datetime(6)`|否|无|下次对账|
 |reconcile_epoch|`bigint unsigned`|否|0|独立对账租约代际|
 |reconcile_owner|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|对账者|
 |reconcile_expires_at|`datetime(6)`|是|无|对账租约DB截止|
 |external_result_id|`longtext`|是|无|外部结果定位|
 |last_error|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|安全错误码|
 |subject_kind|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|聚合类型|
 |subject_id|`varchar(255) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin`|否|无|聚合ID|
 |current_execution_no|`bigint unsigned`|否|无|当前执行代际；初次1|
+|reconcile_scan_seq|`bigint`|否|无|服务器 AUTO_INCREMENT 分配的正 Java long 扫描序号；持久化后应用不可改写；无显式默认是服务器分配例外，写入省略本列|
 
 主键：`id`。
 - 唯一约束 `external_key(operation_kind,business_key)`：同类型业务键唯一。
 - 索引 `external_due(state,next_check_at,id)`：到期对账。
+- 唯一约束 `external_reconcile_scan(reconcile_scan_seq)`：不可变序号原始 keyset 扫描与固定高水位。
 - CHECK `ck_external_operation_0`：`state IN ('MARKED','UNKNOWN','RETRY_QUEUED','SUCCEEDED','FAILED','MANUAL_REQUIRED')`。
 - CHECK `ck_external_operation_1`：`current_execution_no > 0`。
 - FK `fk_external_operation_0`：`subject_kind,subject_id` → `task_subject(subject_kind,subject_id)`。同一事务校验同会议/同所有者；删除 RESTRICT，先显式解除子引用。
 
-更新/删除与完整性：operation_kind/business_key全历史唯一；不归单个task。current_execution_no由同聚合锁下CAS；独立reconcile_epoch跨执行永久递增、不清零。当前执行由(operation_id,no)应用校验。仅FAILED且当前execution有确定零效果证据可追加执行；UNKNOWN禁止重发。
+更新/删除与完整性：operation_kind/business_key全历史唯一；不归单个task。current_execution_no由同聚合锁下CAS；独立reconcile_epoch跨执行永久递增、不清零。当前执行由(operation_id,no)应用校验。仅FAILED且当前execution有确定零效果证据可追加执行；UNKNOWN禁止重发；reconcile_scan_seq仅作有界枚举元数据，不授予任务/发送权限；服务器分配正long，应用持久化后不改写；受控恢复保留映射并完整校验正值/唯一性。
 
 ## task_outbox_sequence
 
 提交序列锁，避免 AUTO_INCREMENT 先提交高号跳漏。所属 B-05，计划 V010。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |id|`int`|否|无|固定1|
 |next_id|`bigint unsigned`|否|1|下一事件号|
 
 主键：`id`。
 - CHECK `ck_task_outbox_sequence_0`：`id = 1`。
 - CHECK `ck_task_outbox_sequence_1`：`next_id > 0`。
 
 更新/删除与完整性：所有业务锁及审计后最后锁定 id=1；分配/插入事件后立即提交，禁止再锁其他领域行。
 
 ## task_outbox
 
 持久引用事件（架构 outbox_event）。所属 B-05，计划 V010。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |sequence_id|`bigint unsigned`|否|无|由事务序列分配的提交顺序|
 |event_type|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|事件类型|
 |task_id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|可空任务引用|
 |subject_kind|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|订阅/鉴权聚合类型|
 |subject_id|`varchar(255) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin`|否|无|聚合ID|
 |subject_revision|`bigint unsigned`|否|无|事件业务版本|
 |reference_kind|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|事件引用类型|
 |reference_id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|不含正文的引用ID|
 |occurred_at|`datetime(6)`|否|无|UTC事件时间|
 
 主键：`sequence_id`。
 - 索引 `outbox_subject(subject_kind,subject_id,sequence_id)`：授权订阅增量。
 - FK `fk_task_outbox_0`：`task_id` → `durable_task(id)`。同一事务校验同会议/同所有者；删除 RESTRICT，先显式解除子引用。
 
 更新/删除与完整性：typed subject/reference 注册器检查；事件不直接携正文；流读取重新鉴权；不按TTL删历史游标，归档后需要明确reset协议。
 
 ## outbox_consumer
 
 持久消费游标及接管 fence。所属 B-05，计划 V010。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |consumer_id|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|稳定消费组|
 |cursor|`bigint unsigned`|否|0|已完成连续前缀|
 |epoch|`bigint unsigned`|否|0|消费者领取代际|
 |lease_owner|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|是|无|消费者|
 |expires_at|`datetime(6)`|是|无|DB租约截止|
 
 主键：`consumer_id`。
 
 更新/删除与完整性：游标只能前进，重放可重复；本地效果与游标同事务；外部消费者以事件序号去重且成功ACK后才推进；拒绝越过失败事件。
 
 ## model_capacity
 
 已审核模型在途容量；无默认公司额度。所属 B-05，计划 V010。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |model|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|模型配置稳定键|
 |capacity|`int unsigned`|否|无|明确容量|
 |config_revision|`bigint unsigned`|否|无|权威配置版本|
 |enabled|`tinyint unsigned`|否|0|允许新调用|
 |cutoff_policy|`varchar(64) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|平台有证据截止或必须人工终结|
 
 主键：`model`。
 - CHECK `ck_model_capacity_0`：`capacity > 0`。
 - CHECK `ck_model_capacity_1`：`enabled IN (0,1)`。
 - CHECK `ck_model_capacity_2`：`cutoff_policy IN ('PROVEN_DEADLINE','EXPLICIT_SETTLEMENT')`。
 
 更新/删除与完整性：版本/快照 INSERT 后不可改写；可变状态仅由所属领域服务 CAS；历史无自动过期；会议删除按依赖逆序显式清理。
 
 ## model_reservation
 
 独立于短任务租约的模型额度保留。所属 B-05，计划 V010。
 
 |字段|MySQL 类型/排序规则|可空|默认|含义|
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 |id|`char(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin`|否|无|内部 UUID；服务器生成，永不复用|

 # AMA 数据库详细设计基线
 
 当前设计版本：**B00-1.5-runtime-metadata**，98表/731列；在完整 B00-1.4-proposed 模型上仅新增两个运行时 sidecar。V013.1 的实际验证仅覆盖这两表及原部署模型不变性；未重跑完整未来模型验证。原 D01 三表与两列仍为 UNASSIGNED-D01，不由此启用。详见[运行时补充与证据](../../acceptance/b/database-runtime-metadata-task-02.md)。
 
 父版本历史（保留）：当前设计版本：**B00-1.4-proposed**，96表/727列；仅设计提案与独立审阅库验证，尚未采用。新增3个规范化来源/解析选择关系及2个可空历史兼容字段。详见[来源补充规格](2026-10-22-ama-material-provenance-addendum.md)及[验证记录](../../acceptance/b/database-material-provenance-addendum-review.md)。正式SQL001..013（含011.1）不可变；未分配正式迁移号，运行时不变。B00-1.3四份原始文件及SHA保存在[历史清单](../../database/design-history/B00-1.3/manifest.json)，原93表实际metadata及raw evidence保持原路径原字节。以下1.3说明保留为历史范围，不授予新方案采用状态。

 1.2 原始状态全文、设计/字典/DDL/声明源字节及SHA已保存于 [design-history/B00-1.2](../../database/design-history/B00-1.2/manifest.json)。原上传补充段标题曾写“待独立评审”，但同版本顶部及采用记录已明确通过；本版只更正当前标题并保留此前原字节，不重签或覆盖1.2评审。
